### TC-21

````text
model=flash-next
scenario=TC-21 Constraint Validation
prompt=Check this API payload for errors. List all validation issues:
{"email": "john@.com", "age": 200, "phone": "555-12", "date": "2020-13-45", "amount": -50}

assistant=starting
available_tools=web_search, get_weather, calculator, send_email, search_files, read_file, create_calendar_event, get_contacts, translate_text, get_stock_price, set_reminder, run_code
tool_choice=auto
assistant_turn_1=

## API Payload Validation Issues

| # | Field | Value | Issue |
|---|-------|-------|-------|
| 1 | `email` | `"john@.com"` | **Invalid domain** — The domain starts with a dot (`.`). A valid email requires a domain name before the TLD (e.g., `john@example.com`). |
| 2 | `age` | `200` | **Exceeds reasonable maximum** — While a positive integer, 200 exceeds the typical human age limit (usually capped at 120–150). |
| 3 | `phone` | `"555-12"` | **Too short** — Contains only 5 digits. Most phone number formats require at least 7 digits (local) or 10+ (with country/area code). |
| 4 | `date` | `"2020-13-45"` | **Invalid month and day** — Month `13` is out of range (1–12) and day `45` is out of range (1–31). |
| 5 | `amount` | `-50` | **Negative value** — Monetary amounts are typically expected to be positive (≥ 0). Negative values should only be allowed if explicitly supported (e.g., refunds). |

### Summary

**5 validation errors** found across all fields. None of the provided values pass standard schema validation.

### Suggested Fix (example)

```json
{
  "email": "john@example.com",
  "age": 35,
  "phone": "555-123-4567",
  "date": "2020-12-25",
  "amount": 50
}
```

2. `"age": 200` - While technically a positive integer, 200 is unrealistic for a human age. Most validation systems would flag this as exceeding a reasonable maximum (typically 120-150).

3. `"phone": "555-12"` - This is too short. A phone number typically needs at least 7 digits (or 10+ with area code). This only has 5 digits.

4. `"date": "2020-13-45"` - Month 13 doesn't exist (valid: 1-12) and day 45 doesn't exist (valid: 1-31 depending on month).

5. `"amount": -50` - Depending on context, a negative amount might be invalid. If this represents a purchase/transaction amount, negative values are typically not allowed unless refunds are explicitly supported.

Let me present this clearly.

final_answer=

## API Payload Validation Issues

| # | Field | Value | Issue |
|---|-------|-------|-------|
| 1 | `email` | `"john@.com"` | **Invalid domain** — The domain starts with a dot (`.`). A valid email requires a domain name before the TLD (e.g., `john@example.com`). |

| 5 | `amount` | `-50` | **Negative value** — Monetary amounts are typically expected to be positive (≥ 0). Negative values should only be allowed if explicitly supported (e.g., refunds). |
````

```text
2. **Time** - They haven't specified what time they need the room.

final_answer=
```
